Kimi:
 Matplotlib не поддерживает `\mathbf` в mathtext. Исправлю — уберу `\mathbf` и `\&`.    Вот полная спецификация **Subcontractor Network v1.0**.
________________________________________
SUBCONTRACTOR NETWORK v1.0: Агент как генеральный подрядчик
________________________________________
1. Проблема
Сценарий	Что происходит	Потери
Агент берёт full-stack проект	Слаб в дизайне, делает плохо	Негативный отзыв, -JSS
Агент отказывает из-за слабости	Упущенная выручка $3000	Месячный бюджет не выполнен
Агент учит новый навык для одного проекта	40 часов обучения, $0 дохода	Невыгодно при разовой задаче
Решение: Агент не выполняет всё сам — он оркестрирует специализированных субподрядчиков, забирая margin.
________________________________________
2. Архитектура: 5 слоёв
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    AGENT-GENERAL                                │
│  Receives project $3000 → Decomposes → Orchestrates → Delivers │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 1: PROJECT DECOMPOSITION                                 │
├──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬────────────────────────────────┤
│ BACKEND  │ FRONTEND │ DESIGN   │ QA / DEVOPS                    │
│ $1200    │ $1000    │ $500     │ $300                           │
│ 40%      │ 33%      │ 17%      │ 10%                            │
└──────────┴──────────┴──────────┴────────────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 2: SUBCONTRACTOR SELECTION & REPUTATION BOND             │
│                                                                 │
│  SUB-A (Backend)  SUB-B (Frontend)  SUB-C (Design)  SUB-D (QA) │
│  $80/hr, JSS 96%  $65/hr, JSS 94%   $45/hr, JSS 98% $35/hr    │
│  Bond: $200       Bond: $150        Bond: $100      Bond: $80  │
│                                                                 │
│  Score: S_i = argmax (JSS × SkillMatch) / (Rate × Risk)        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 3: ORCHESTRATION & SYNCHRONIZATION                     │
│  Sync Schedule → Interface Contracts → Quality Gates → Escalation│
│  (dependency graph, API specs, code review, conflict resolution)│
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 4: MARGIN EXTRACTION & RISK MANAGEMENT                  │
│                                                                 │
│  BREAK EVEN:  Revenue $3000, Costs $3000, Margin $0            │
│  REALISTIC:   Revenue $3000, Costs $2550, Margin $450 (15%)    │
│  OPTIMAL:     Revenue $3600, Costs $2550, Margin $1050 (29%)   │
│                                                                 │
│  Risk: Quality (bond), Timeline (penalty), Scope (change fee) │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 5: DELIVERY & REPUTATION ATTRIBUTION                     │
│  Integrate → End-to-End Test → Deliver → Shapley Attribution → Payment│
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
________________________________________
3. Полная математика
Subcontractor score:
S_i=(JSS_i⋅SkillMatch_i)/(Rate_i⋅(1+Risk_i ) )
Reputation bond:
B_i=min(0.2⋅V_i,$500)
Agent margin:
M=V_client-∑_i^▒c_i ⋅V_i-C_overhead, c_i∈[0.75,0.90]
Shapley attribution (для репутации):
φ_i=∑_(S⊆N\{i})^▒(|S|!⋅(n-|S|-1)!)/n! [V(S∪{i})-V(S)]
Risk-adjusted return:
RAR=M/σ_delivery , σ_delivery=√(∑_i^▒p_i^fail ⋅(V_i )^2 )
________________________________________
4. Реализация: полный код
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Callable
import numpy as np
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass
class Subcontractor:
    id: str
    name: str
    skills: List[str]  # ["python", "django", "postgresql"]
    hourly_rate: float
    jss: float  # 0-1
    availability: float  # 0-1, % времени доступен
    past_projects: int
    avg_rating: float  # 1-5
    bond_deposit: float  # текущий депозит
    
    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Sub({self.name}, ${self.hourly_rate}/hr, JSS={self.jss:.0%})"


@dataclass
class WorkPackage:
    name: str
    description: str
    required_skills: List[str]
    estimated_hours: float
    budget_share: float  # % от общего бюджета
    dependencies: List[str]  # какие пакеты должны быть готовы до этого
    deliverables: List[str]
    
    def __repr__(self):
        return f"WP({self.name}, ${self.budget_share:.0f}, {self.estimated_hours}h)"


class ProjectDecomposer:
    """LAYER 1: Разбиваем проект на work packages"""
    
    # Шаблоны декомпозиции по типам проектов
    DECOMPOSITION_TEMPLATES = {
        'fullstack_web': [
            WorkPackage('backend', 'API + DB', ['python', 'django'], 40, 0.40, [], ['api_spec', 'db_schema']),
            WorkPackage('frontend', 'UI + UX', ['react', 'typescript'], 35, 0.33, ['backend'], ['components', 'tests']),
            WorkPackage('design', 'Mockups', ['figma', 'ui_ux'], 15, 0.17, [], ['mockups', 'styleguide']),
            WorkPackage('qa_devops', 'Testing + Deploy', ['docker', 'ci_cd'], 10, 0.10, ['frontend', 'backend'], ['tests', 'deployed_app'])
        ],
        'mobile_app': [
            WorkPackage('backend', 'API + DB', ['node', 'mongodb'], 30, 0.35, [], ['api']),
            WorkPackage('mobile', 'iOS/Android', ['react_native', 'swift'], 50, 0.45, ['backend'], ['app_build']),
            WorkPackage('design', 'Mobile UI', ['figma'], 15, 0.15, [], ['mobile_mockups']),
            WorkPackage('qa', 'Testing', ['appium'], 10, 0.10, ['mobile'], ['test_report'])
        ],
        'data_pipeline': [
            WorkPackage('ingestion', 'Data sources', ['python', 'airflow'], 25, 0.30, [], ['ingestion_jobs']),
            WorkPackage('transformation', 'ETL logic', ['spark', 'sql'], 35, 0.35, ['ingestion'], ['transformed_data']),
            WorkPackage('visualization', 'Dashboards', ['tableau', 'sql'], 20, 0.20, ['transformation'], ['dashboards']),
            WorkPackage('infrastructure', 'Cloud setup', ['aws', 'terraform'], 15, 0.15, [], ['deployed_pipeline'])
        ]
    }
    
    def decompose(self, project_type: str, total_budget: float) -> List[WorkPackage]:
        template = self.DECOMPOSITION_TEMPLATES.get(project_type, self.DECOMPOSITION_TEMPLATES['fullstack_web'])
        
        # Адаптируем бюджеты под реальный total
        packages = []
        for wp in template:
            adapted = WorkPackage(
                name=wp.name,
                description=wp.description,
                required_skills=wp.required_skills,
                estimated_hours=wp.estimated_hours,
                budget_share=wp.budget_share * total_budget,
                dependencies=wp.dependencies,
                deliverables=wp.deliverables
            )
            packages.append(adapted)
        
        return packages


class SubcontractorSelector:
    """LAYER 2: Выбираем субподрядчиков"""
    
    def __init__(self, subcontractor_pool: List[Subcontractor]):
        self.pool = subcontractor_pool
        self.bond_escrow = {}  # sub_id -> bond_amount
    
    def score(self, sub: Subcontractor, wp: WorkPackage) -> float:
        """Score = (JSS × SkillMatch) / (Rate × Risk)"""
        
        # Skill match: сколько навыков совпадает
        skill_match = len(set(sub.skills) & set(wp.required_skills)) / len(wp.required_skills)
        
        # Risk: inverse of past success
        risk = 1 - (sub.avg_rating / 5) * (sub.past_projects / (sub.past_projects + 10))
        
        return (sub.jss * skill_match) / (sub.hourly_rate * (1 + risk))
    
    def select(self, wp: WorkPackage, n_candidates: int = 3) -> List[Dict]:
        """Возвращаем ранжированный список кандидатов"""
        
        candidates = []
        for sub in self.pool:
            # Проверяем доступность
            if sub.availability < 0.3:  # менее 30% доступен
                continue
            
            # Проверяем skill overlap
            if not set(sub.skills) & set(wp.required_skills):
                continue
            
            score = self.score(sub, wp)
            estimated_cost = sub.hourly_rate * wp.estimated_hours
            
            candidates.append({
                'subcontractor': sub,
                'score': score,
                'estimated_cost': estimated_cost,
                'skill_match': len(set(sub.skills) & set(wp.required_skills)) / len(wp.required_skills)
            })
        
        # Ранжируем по score
        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)
        return candidates[:n_candidates]
    
    def require_bond(self, sub: Subcontractor, wp: WorkPackage) -> float:
        """Требуем reputation bond"""
        bond = min(0.2 * wp.budget_share, 500)  # max $500
        self.bond_escrow[sub.id] = bond
        return bond
    
    def release_bond(self, sub_id: str, success: bool):
        """Возвращаем или конфискуем bond"""
        bond = self.bond_escrow.get(sub_id, 0)
        
        if success:
            # Возвращаем + bonus за хорошую работу
            return bond * 1.1
        else:
            # Конфискуем
            return 0


class Orchestrator:
    """LAYER 3: Синхронизация и контроль качества"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.schedule = {}
        self.interface_contracts = {}
        self.quality_gates = []
    
    def create_schedule(self, packages: List[WorkPackage], assignments: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Строим dependency graph и critical path"""
        
        # Topological sort по dependencies
        schedule = {}
        completed = set()
        
        while len(completed) < len(packages):
            for wp in packages:
                if wp.name in completed:
                    continue
                
                # Все зависимости выполнены?
                if all(dep in completed for dep in wp.dependencies):
                    sub = assignments[wp.name]
                    start_time = max(
                        (schedule[dep]['end'] for dep in wp.dependencies),
                        default=0
                    )
                    end_time = start_time + wp.estimated_hours / sub.availability
                    
                    schedule[wp.name] = {
                        'sub': sub,
                        'start': start_time,
                        'end': end_time,
                        'status': 'scheduled'
                    }
                    completed.add(wp.name)
        
        return schedule
    
    def define_interface(self, wp1: WorkPackage, wp2: WorkPackage):
        """Определяем контракт между пакетами"""
        # API spec, data formats, delivery criteria
        self.interface_contracts[(wp1.name, wp2.name)] = {
            'input_format': 'JSON',
            'auth_method': 'JWT',
            'response_time_sla': 500,  # ms
            'error_rate_sla': 0.01     # 1%
        }
    
    def quality_gate(self, wp: WorkPackage, deliverables: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Проверяем deliverables перед передачей следующему пакету"""
        
        checks = [
            len(deliverables) >= len(wp.deliverables),
            all(d in deliverables for d in wp.deliverables),
            # Code review passed
            # Tests pass
            # Documentation complete
        ]
        
        return all(checks)
    
    def handle_conflict(self, wp: WorkPackage, issue: str):
        """Эскалация при конфликте"""
        strategies = {
            'delay': 'Allocate more time, adjust schedule',
            'quality_fail': 'Request rework, apply bond penalty',
            'sub_unavailable': 'Replace with standby sub',
            'scope_dispute': 'Invoke change order process'
        }
        return strategies.get(issue, 'Escalate to agent-general')


class MarginCalculator:
    """LAYER 4: Считаем P&L"""
    
    def __init__(self, overhead_rate: float = 0.15):
        self.overhead_rate = overhead_rate  # 15% на управление
    
    def calculate(self, project_value: float, 
                  assignments: Dict[str, Dict],
                  fee_structure: str = 'markup') -> Dict:
        """
        fee_structure:
          - 'pass_through': агент берёт 0%, просто координирует (для JSS)
          - 'markup': агент добавляет 15-20% к costs
          - 'management_fee': агент берёт 20-30% сверху у клиента
        """
        
        # Стоимость субподрядчиков (они берут 75-90% от allocated budget)
        sub_costs = sum(
            info['estimated_cost'] * 0.85  # средний коэффициент
            for info in assignments.values()
        )
        
        # Overhead: время агента на управление
        management_hours = sum(wp['estimated_hours'] for wp in assignments.values()) * 0.15
        overhead_cost = management_hours * 50  # $50/hr agent time
        
        total_costs = sub_costs + overhead_cost
        
        if fee_structure == 'pass_through':
            revenue = total_costs
            margin = 0
        elif fee_structure == 'markup':
            revenue = total_costs * 1.15
            margin = revenue - total_costs
        elif fee_structure == 'management_fee':
            revenue = project_value  # клиент платит больше
            margin = project_value - total_costs
        
        return {
            'revenue': revenue,
            'sub_costs': sub_costs,
            'overhead': overhead_cost,
            'total_costs': total_costs,
            'margin': margin,
            'margin_pct': margin / revenue if revenue > 0 else 0,
            'risk_adjusted_margin': margin - self._risk_premium(assignments)
        }
    
    def _risk_premium(self, assignments: Dict) -> float:
        """Резерв на риски"""
        # 10% от стоимости на случай проблем
        return sum(info['estimated_cost'] for info in assignments.values()) * 0.10


class DeliveryManager:
    """LAYER 5: Доставка и атрибуция репутации"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.shapley_calculator = ShapleyAttributor()
    
    def integrate(self, deliverables: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> Dict:
        """Собираем всё воедино"""
        integrated = {
            'components': deliverables,
            'integration_tests': [],
            'documentation': []
        }
        
        # End-to-end testing
        # ...
        
        return integrated
    
    def attribute_reputation(self, project_success: float, 
                             assignments: Dict) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Shapley value: кто сколько внёс в успех проекта"""
        
        # Упрощённо: пропорционально skill match × quality of deliverables
        contributions = {}
        for wp_name, info in assignments.items():
            contributions[wp_name] = info['skill_match'] * info['quality_score']
        
        # Нормализуем
        total = sum(contributions.values())
        return {k: v/total for k, v in contributions.items()}
    
    def distribute_payment(self, project_value: float, 
                           assignments: Dict,
                           attribution: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Распределяем оплату с учётом attribution"""
        
        payments = {}
        for wp_name, info in assignments.items():
            base = info['estimated_cost']
            bonus = project_value * 0.1 * attribution.get(wp_name, 0)  # 10% бонусный пул
            payments[info['subcontractor'].id] = base + bonus
        
        return payments


class SubcontractorNetwork:
    """Единый интерфейс"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.decomposer = ProjectDecomposer()
        self.selector = SubcontractorSelector([])
        self.orchestrator = Orchestrator()
        self.margin_calc = MarginCalculator()
        self.delivery = DeliveryManager()
        
        self.subcontractor_pool = []
        self.project_history = []
    
    def add_subcontractor(self, sub: Subcontractor):
        self.subcontractor_pool.append(sub)
        self.selector.pool = self.subcontractor_pool
    
    def execute_project(self, project_type: str, total_budget: float,
                        client_requirements: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Полный pipeline: от проекта к доставке"""
        
        # LAYER 1: Decompose
        packages = self.decomposer.decompose(project_type, total_budget)
        
        # LAYER 2: Select subcontractors
        assignments = {}
        for wp in packages:
            candidates = self.selector.select(wp)
            if not candidates:
                return {'status': 'FAILED', 'reason': f'No sub for {wp.name}'}
            
            best = candidates[0]
            bond = self.selector.require_bond(best['subcontractor'], wp)
            
            assignments[wp.name] = {
                'subcontractor': best['subcontractor'],
                'estimated_cost': best['estimated_cost'],
                'skill_match': best['skill_match'],
                'bond': bond,
                'quality_score': 0  # заполняется позже
            }
        
        # LAYER 3: Orchestrate
        schedule = self.orchestrator.create_schedule(packages, assignments)
        
        # LAYER 4: Calculate margin
        pnl = self.margin_calc.calculate(total_budget, assignments, 'management_fee')
        
        if pnl['margin'] < 0:
            return {'status': 'DECLINED', 'reason': 'Negative margin', 'pnl': pnl}
        
        # Execute (в реальности — асинхронно с мониторингом)
        # ... execution logic ...
        
        # LAYER 5: Deliver
        deliverables = {wp.name: wp.deliverables for wp in packages}
        integrated = self.delivery.integrate(deliverables)
        
        # Attribution
        attribution = self.delivery.attribute_reputation(1.0, assignments)
        payments = self.delivery.distribute_payment(total_budget, assignments, attribution)
        
        # Release bonds
        for wp_name, info in assignments.items():
            success = info['quality_score'] > 0.8
            returned = self.selector.release_bond(info['subcontractor'].id, success)
        
        result = {
            'status': 'COMPLETED',
            'pnl': pnl,
            'schedule': schedule,
            'attribution': attribution,
            'payments': payments,
            'margin': pnl['margin']
        }
        
        self.project_history.append(result)
        return result

# Создаём сеть
network = SubcontractorNetwork()

    hourly_rate=80, jss=0.96, availability=0.7,

))

    id='sub_b', name='Maria (Frontend)',
    skills=['react', 'typescript', 'tailwind'],
    hourly_rate=65, jss=0.94, availability=0.8,
    past_projects=32, avg_rating=4.8, bond_deposit=0
))

    skills=['figma', 'ui_ux', 'prototyping'],

8. Ограничения и риски
Риск	Митигация
Subcontractor провалил задачу	Reputation bond + standby sub
Subcontractor исчез	Bond конфискация + резервный
Качество ниже ожиданий	Quality gates + code review
Клиент узнал о субподряде	Прозрачность: «моя команда»
Margin съела коммуникация	Async-first, шаблонизированные апдейты
Minimum viable project: $1500. Ниже — проще выполнить самому, overhead на coordination не окупается.

________________________________________
SKILL DEMAND FORECASTING v1.0: Прогнозирование рынка навыков
________________________________________
1. Проблема
Сценарий	Что происходит	Потери
Агент — эксперт в React	18 месяцев спустя спрос падает	Выручка -40%, конкуренция растёт
Агент учит новый навык	Делает это после того, как спрос вырос	Поздно, рынок уже переполнен
Агент не следит за трендами	Продолжает делать устаревшие вещи	Репутация «legacy developer»
Решение: Предсказывать сдвиги спроса за 3-6 месяцев и переквалифицироваться заранее.

2. Архитектура: 5 слоёв
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 1: DATA SOURCES (Multi-Signal Fusion)                    │
├─────────┬─────────┬─────────┬──────────┬────────────────────────┤
│ JOB     │ GITHUB  │ HN/     │ GOOGLE   │ COURSES                │

                              │
                              ▼

├─────────────┬─────────────┬─────────────┬──────────┬────────────┤
│ DEMAND      │ SUPPLY        │ PRICE       │ SENTIMENT│ TECH       │

                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 3: FORECASTING MODEL (Ensemble: Prophet + LSTM + VAR)  │
│                                                                 │
│  ŷ_{t+h} = f(Prophet_trend, LSTM_pattern, VAR_cross-skill) + ε  │
│                                                                 │
│  Prophet: Trend + seasonality + holidays                        │
│  LSTM: Sequential patterns + long memory                        │
│  VAR: Cross-skill correlations + Granger causality              │
│  Ensemble: Weighted average + uncertainty bands                 │
│                                                                 │
│  Uncertainty: ŷ_{t+h} ± 1.96·σ_h (95% CI)                      │
│  σ_h = α·h^β (grows with horizon)                               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  LAYER 4: SIGNAL CLASSIFICATION (Portfolio Theory for Skills)  │
├──────────┬────────────┬─────────────┬──────────────────────────┤
│  HOLD    │ ACCUMULATE │ SPECULATE   │ REDUCE                   │
├──────────┼────────────┼─────────────┼──────────────────────────┤
│ Mature,  │ Early      │ Niche, high │ Declining                │
│ stable   │ growth,    │ volatility  │ demand                   │
│ demand   │ low comp.  │ Small pos.  │ Harvest value            │
│          │            │ High margin │ Don't invest             │
│ React    │ Svelte     │ Zig, Mojo   │ jQuery, PHP legacy       │
│ Python   │ Rust, WASM │ WebGPU      │ Flash                    │
│ AWS      │            │             │                          │
└──────────┴────────────┴─────────────┴──────────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼

│  LAYER 5: ADAPTIVE STRATEGY                                     │
├────────────────┬────────────────┬───────────────┬───────────────┤
│ LEARNING       │ PORTFOLIO      │ PRICING       │ CONTENT       │
│ BUDGET         │ REBALANCE      │ STRATEGY      │ STRATEGY      │
├────────────────┼────────────────┼───────────────┼───────────────┤
│ 20% for ACCUM. │ Monthly review │ HOLD: market  │ Publish ACCUM.│

│ 70% for HOLD   │ Kelly criterion│ ACCUM: -15%   │ Build auth.   │
│ (revenue)      │ for learning   │ SPEC: +50%    │ SEO emerging  │

3. Полная математика
Demand score:
D_s (t)=α⋅jobs_s (t)+β⋅mentions_s (t)+γ⋅rate_growth_s (t)
Supply score:
S_s (t)=δ⋅freelancers_s (t)+ϵ⋅courses_s (t)
Market pressure (demand-to-supply ratio):
P_s (t)=(D_s (t))/(S_s (t)+1)
Forecast:
P ̂_s (t+h)="Ensemble" ("Prophet" ,"LSTM" ,"VAR" )" with 95%CI" 
Signal classification:
〖"Signal" 〗_s={■("HOLD" &P ̂_s " stable,high confidence" @"ACCUMULATE" &P ̂_s↑," medium confidence" @"SPECULATE" &P ̂_s " volatile,low confidence" @"REDUCE" &P ̂_s↓," high confidence" )┤
Learning allocation (Kelly criterion):
L_s=Kelly(P_s,confidence)/(∑_k^▒K elly(P_k,confidence) )⋅L_total
________________________________________
4. Реализация: полный код
```python from dataclasses import dataclass from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple import numpy as np import pandas as pd from datetime import datetime, timedelta from collections import defaultdict
@dataclass class SkillSignal: skill: str demand_score: float supply_score: float market_pressure: float forecast_3m: float forecast_6m: float confidence: float # 0-1 signal: str # HOLD, ACCUMULATE, SPECULATE, REDUCE trend_direction: str # UP, DOWN, STABLE, VOLATILE
class DataCollector: “““LAYER 1: Собираем данные из множества источников”“”
def __init__(self):
    self.cache = {}
    self.last_update = {}

def collect_job_boards(self, skill: str, days: int = 90) -> Dict:
    """Indeed, LinkedIn, Upwork, Toptal"""
    # В реальности — API calls или scraping
    # Здесь — заглушка с realistic данными
    return {
        'job_postings': np.random.poisson(50 + np.sin(days/30) * 20),
        'avg_budget': np.random.normal(2000, 500),
        'hire_rate': np.random.beta(3, 2),
        'time_to_fill': np.random.normal(14, 5)
    }

def collect_github(self, skill: str, days: int = 90) -> Dict:
    """GitHub API: repos, stars, PR velocity"""
    return {
        'new_repos': np.random.poisson(100),
        'stars_growth': np.random.normal(0.15, 0.05),  # 15% monthly
        'pr_velocity': np.random.normal(5, 2),  # PRs per week
        'contributor_growth': np.random.normal(0.1, 0.03)
    }

def collect_sentiment(self, skill: str, days: int = 90) -> Dict:
    """Hacker News, Reddit, Twitter sentiment"""
    return {
        'hn_score': np.random.normal(50, 20),
        'hn_sentiment': np.random.normal(0.2, 0.3),  # -1 to 1
        'reddit_mentions': np.random.poisson(30),
        'twitter_sentiment': np.random.normal(0.1, 0.2),
        'so_questions': np.random.poisson(100)
    }

def collect_google_trends(self, skill: str, days: int = 90) -> pd.Series:
    """Google Trends: search volume"""
    # Симуляция тренда
    t = np.arange(days)
    trend = 50 + 20 * np.sin(t / 30) + np.random.normal(0, 5, days)
    return pd.Series(trend, index=pd.date_range(end=datetime.now(), periods=days))

def collect_courses(self, skill: str) -> Dict:
    """Udemy, Coursera, Pluralsight"""
    return {
        'course_count': np.random.poisson(20),
        'enrollment_growth': np.random.normal(0.25, 0.1),
        'completion_rate': np.random.beta(2, 3),
        'avg_rating': np.random.normal(4.2, 0.3)
    }

def get_all_signals(self, skill: str) -> Dict:
    """Объединяем все источники"""
    if skill in self.cache and (datetime.now() - self.last_update.get(skill, datetime.min)).days < 1:
        return self.cache[skill]
    
    signals = {
        'job_boards': self.collect_job_boards(skill),
        'github': self.collect_github(skill),
        'sentiment': self.collect_sentiment(skill),
        'google_trends': self.collect_google_trends(skill),
        'courses': self.collect_courses(skill),
        'timestamp': datetime.now()
    }
    
    self.cache[skill] = signals
    self.last_update[skill] = datetime.now()
    return signals
class FeatureEngineer: “““LAYER 2: Преобразуем сырые данные в признаки”“”
def __init__(self):
    self.skill_history = defaultdict(list)

def extract_features(self, skill: str, signals: Dict) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Извлекаем нормализованные признаки"""
    
    # Demand signals
    job_postings = signals['job_boards']['job_postings']
    mentions = signals['sentiment']['reddit_mentions'] + signals['sentiment']['so_questions']
    rate_growth = (signals['job_boards']['avg_budget'] - 1500) / 1500  # нормализация
    
    # Supply signals
    freelancers = signals['courses']['enrollment_growth'] * 1000  # proxy
    courses = signals['courses']['course_count']
    
    # Price signals
    avg_rate = signals['job_boards']['avg_budget'] / 40  # примерно hourly
    bid_competition = 1 / (signals['job_boards']['hire_rate'] + 0.01)
    
    # Sentiment signals
    hn_score = signals['sentiment']['hn_score']
    sentiment = signals['sentiment']['hn_sentiment']
    
    # Tech maturity
    version_releases = signals['github']['pr_velocity']
    adoption_curve = signals['github']['stars_growth']
    
    features = {
        'job_postings': job_postings,
        'mentions': mentions,
        'rate_growth': rate_growth,
        'freelancers': freelancers,
        'courses': courses,
        'avg_rate': avg_rate,
        'bid_competition': bid_competition,
        'hn_score': hn_score
